# Epic encounters: what the scripts do, and what bots would have to do

Generated by `source/tools/epic_encounter_catalog.py` from the boss scripts in `source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/` and the hand checks in `docs/encounters/hand-notes.json`. Don't edit this file: edit the hand notes and run the generator (`--check` tells CI whether it is up to date). The machine-readable version is [epic-encounters.json](epic-encounters.json).

**This is a catalog, step 1 of "bots play epic encounters properly". Nothing in it is implemented.** Every tactic is a proposal. Darkness Falls raid AI, Legion and the hardest level 70+ encounters have no raid AI; they are listed, marked *no raid AI*. No rule here was checked in game: "verified" means a person read the rule against its script.

- Scripts read: 208. Encounters: 244 (228 with rules, 2 written by hand).
- Rules: 850; verified against the script: 850; confidence read directly: 491, inferred: 359.
- By kind: adds 149, aggro_wipe 11, damage_reduced 3, empowered 15, hazard 3, heal 26, immune_while 66, linked_pack 119, offline_change 23, pet_devour 2, phase 54, position_puzzle 10, reflect 8, reset 277, teleport 30, tether 54.

Difficulty: *easy for the current raid AI* (it would do this with small changes, or already does), *needs new raid AI* (target choice, splitting, timing the raid doesn't have), *unsafe for bots today* (bots can't recover from it yet), *off today* (an `EpicEncounterMechanics` switch disables it, or the script only ever picks real players: `GameBot` is a `GameNPC`, not a `GamePlayer`, so `GetPlayersInRadius` and `is GamePlayer` never choose a bot).

## Encounters with raid tactics

The raid AI (`EpicEncounterTactics`, autonomous gamebots only) plays these tactics, read from the hand notes' `aiTactics` into `EpicEncounterTactics.Catalog.cs`. Tested by unit and harness tests only; not yet seen in an overnight run (its log lines: `REALM_EXPEDITION_TACTIC`, `EPIC_TACTIC_BOT`).

| Encounter | Tactic |
|---|---|
| Xanxicar (Crystal Cave) | Blasts: within 3% above 80/60/40/20% and for 9 s after crossing one, everyone except the party he is fighting withdraws 2,700 from him; that party pushes him over and takes the 2,500-radius stomp (it is meant to kill whoever is in it), the rest of the raid survives it and comes back. |
| Aidon the Archwizard (Hall of the Corrupt) | Required adds, keep one: once the fight is on the raid kills three of his four illusions (each party takes one) and leaves the last alive, because he summons a new set only when all four are dead; then everyone fights him. Fight at home: past 1,100 from his spawn (tether 1,500) the raid stops hitting him and leads him back. |
| Orylle (Krondon) | Ground hazard: Orshom's Fire, as for Orshom Brong (raiders with a Cold spell shoot it from outside its 300, everyone else stays out of it and fights the twins). |
| Orshom Brong (Krondon) | Ground hazard: Orshom's Fire (a fire pit he lights when his fight starts, 10,000 health, hit only by Cold) burns everything within 300 every 3 s and draws everyone within 2,500 into a fight with it. It is never the raid's target: raiders with a Cold spell shoot it from outside its 300, everyone else stays out of it and fights Orshom Brong and Orylle. |
| Scurceol Hyrde (Krondon) | Required adds: the four Lyft Miht orbs are the raid's targets while any lives (each party takes one; fought from a floor spot within 1,400 that the raid can reach and that sees the orb; melee that can't reach a flying orb waits at that spot); nobody hits him until the last orb is dead. Fight at home: past 1,500 from his spawn the raid stops hitting him and the raider he chases leads him back. |
| Ancient Black Oak (Marfach Caverns) | Dies with Black Lady: while she lives the raid fights her instead of the Oak. |
| Black Lady (Marfach Caverns) | Fight at home: past 1,100 from her spawn (her tether is 1,500) the raid stops hitting her and the raider she chases leads her back, so she is never leashed and healed. |
| Nosdoden (Trollheim) | Waves as required adds: when a wave of essences spawns (every 10% from 90%), the raid stops hitting him and kills the wave, then goes back to him; so his health falls one step at a time and waves don't stack. |

## Restored behind switches

Where the fork had changed a mechanic for bots with no switch, the original is back behind an `EpicEncounterMechanics` switch, off by default (the game plays as before). Originals read from OpenDAoC/OpenDAoC-Core 3513bad49912 (2026-11-04) and OpenDAoC/OpenDAoC-Database 7068aef09830, cloned 2026-11-05. Upstream has been refactored since this fork's base (instance fields instead of statics, ProcessDeath), so the behaviour was read, not copied.

| Encounter | Places | Original | Fork today | Switch |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| caer-sidi/fames, caer-sidi/morbus, caer-sidi/funus | 3 | Out of its tether a horseman heals to full and drops its aggro. | It drops its aggro and keeps its health (heals only when the fight is over). | `AllowHorsemenTetherHeal` |
| caer-sidi/funus | 1 | Only clerics, and mercenaries with a short bow, armsmen and infiltrators with a crossbow, scouts with a longbow damage Funus; any other attacker (pets too) heals him to full. | Everyone damages him. | `AllowFunusWeaponPuzzle` |
| caer-sidi/skeletalsacristan | 2 | He walks his loop without stopping (195-250 in this fork's base; upstream now a 220 path) with 70% magic resists. | He walks at 130-160, stands still while hit, 55% magic resists. | `AllowSacristanPatrol` |
| caer-sidi/soulreckoner | 2 | No damage at all while a reckoned soul lives, or outside a 900 room. | A quarter of the damage while a soul lives; the room is 1,500. | `AllowSoulReckonerFullShield, AllowSoulReckonerSmallRoom` |
| galladoria/aroon | 9 | The nine Scaiths die in order (slash, thrust, crush, body, cold, energy, heat, matter, spirit): each is immune until the one before is dead, and Aroon takes only the damage types whose Scaith has died (nothing at first). | Any damage type hurts Aroon and every Scaith. | `AllowAroonDamageOrder` |
| galladoria/olcasgeaninitializator | 1 | The primals come when a real player (not a bot) stands within 350 of the bridge spot. | A raid bot within 1,500 of the spot starts it (players still 350). | `AllowGalladoriaBridgeSpot (bots within 350; a bot-only rule would be unwinnable)` |
| galladoria/olcasgean | 1 | Split-position and throw puzzle. | Off. | `AllowPositionPuzzle (already a switch; nothing new)` |
| galladoria/fireprimal | 1 | Trail of fire: 220 damage in a 450 radius. | 120 in 300. | `AllowFullFirePatches` |
| galladoria/earthprimal | 4 | Each Earthmender takes damage only from its class group (Guardian: blademaster, hero, champion; Magical: eldritch, enchanter, mentalist; Natural: bard, druid, warden; Shadowy: nightshade, ranger; animists, valewalkers and pets hurt all four); they never attack; they heal to full 30 s after a fight; they heal each other and the primal. | Everyone hurts them; they fight back; no quick reset while raiders are in the room; they heal the primal only. | `AllowEarthmenderClassPuzzle, AllowEarthmendersPassive, AllowEarthmenderQuickReset, AllowEarthmenderMutualHeal` |
| galladoria/beatha | 1 | Beatha's damage heals Xaga by twice the amount. | No heal. | `AllowBeathaHealsXaga` |
| galladoria/beatha, galladoria/tine | 2 | The fairies circle the room (250, flying) even while fought. | They hold still while fought. | `AllowFairyFlight` |
| summoner-s-hall/grandsummonergovannon | 1 | 300,000 health. | 200,000. | `AllowGovannonFullHealth` |
| tuscaran-glacier/kvasir | 1 | Cold damage heals him a fifth of his health and does nothing else (upstream has no out-of-tether immunity for him). | Cold hurts him. | `AllowKvasirColdHeal` |
| tuscaran-glacier/queenkula, tuscaran-glacier/kingtuscar | 2 | Damage to one twin heals the other by the same amount while it is below full health. | No heal. | `AllowTuscarTwinHeal` |

**Scurceol's orbs upstream:** Upstream's Lyft Miht orbs are the same: level 90, 15,000 health, 40/50% resists, AF 250, flying at the same four corners. Upstream only replaced the static orb counters with a per-boss list (a bug fix: our static counters are shared by every Scurceol and can drift).

**Helper packs upstream:** OpenDAoC-Database mob.sql compared with the 0.37b release world: of the 47 packages empty in our world, 42 are empty upstream too (the script looks for helpers that never existed: DebenBaf, FamesBaf, MorbusBaf, HatiBaf, SkollBaf, ApocBaf ...). Five have upstream spawns our world lacks: AncientOakBaf (14 "Ancient Oak" in Black Lady's room, none in ours), CaimheulBaf (6 reanimated guardians; 1 in ours, under PackageID offline-classic-frontier-dungeon-restored), CailleachUragaigBaf (3 cailleach guards), RevenantBaf (11 wraiths and wights by the Raumarik Revenant), RunilBaf (4 clan deserters). Not changed here: adding world spawns is a world update for the owner to decide.

## Priority: encounters that block raids today

### 1. Scurceol Hyrde (Krondon): immune while his four orbs live

**Run evidence:** docs/NIGHT-SIEGES-AND-MARFACH.md §1b: build 8d7baa3, 2026-11-05 14:27-15:40. 59-77 of 101 raiders fought him 3,219 units from his spawn; he stayed at 100%; all 42 raider deaths were to him; he was skipped every 20 minutes and switched back to; the Beran muster stalled at 2-5 of 50. 'Lyft' is in no log line. 2026-11-06 (168f55d): the tactic fired at 01:49 and 02:16, but every orb failed the ground-route gate and was set aside; the raid went to Fuladl.

**Why it blocks:** No damage is possible until the orbs die, and the raid never targets them: the orbs never attack, so the 'fighting the raid' switch never picks them, and melee can't reach them.

**Proposed tactic:** Fight him in his own room, not 3,000 units out where the raid met him: one tank party holds him near his spawn while four ranged parties take one orb each from the floor below it (the route already shoots flyers from below), then everyone on him. Orb kills survive his resets.

Rules of Scurceol Hyrde (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Krondon/ScurceolHyrde.cs`):

- **immune_while** (verified): Takes no damage of any type while any of the four Lyft Miht orbs (LyftMihtOne-Four, static counters Orb1Count-Orb4Count) is alive. `ScurceolHyrde.cs:33`. *needs new raid AI*: In the raid AI (EpicEncounterTactics, required adds): while an orb lives the raid's target is an orb, each party takes the orb its index points at, casters and archers shoot it from the floor, melee stands off under it, and nobody hits him. Then the whole raid turns on him.
- **adds** (verified): Spawns his four Lyft Miht orbs when he spawns, one per room corner, only if that orb's counter is 0; they never respawn by themselves. `ScurceolHyrde.cs:132`. *needs new raid AI*: Count orb kills as permanent progress: a wipe or reset on Scurceol does not undo them.
- **tether** (verified): Tether range 3800: drawn further from its spawn it walks home. `ScurceolHyrde.cs:97`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Fight it where it stands; don't pull it past its tether.
- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health `ScurceolHyrde.cs:171`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **teleport** (verified): An orb hit by a real player has a 25% chance (once per pick) to throw that player, 8-15 s later, to one of four spots on the floor and deal 20% of their health as falling damage. The same code is in all four orb brains. `ScurceolHyrde.cs:684`. *off today*: Off for bots today: it picks only real players (GameBot is a GameNPC, not a GamePlayer). To play it: a thrown bot walks back to its orb party.
- **empowered** (verified): Strength 320 instead of 260 while any orb lives (he hits harder until the orbs die). `ScurceolHyrde.cs:180`. *needs new raid AI*: Another reason to kill the orbs first; the tank party needs extra healing until then.

### 2. Black Lady and her Ancient Black Oak (Marfach Caverns)

**Run evidence:** docs/NIGHT-SIEGES-AND-MARFACH.md §1: 2026-11-05 01:28-05:19, 91 pulls on the Oak, never in combat, 0 raiders within 1,000; Black Lady leashed home from 44% after being drawn 1,618 from her spawn. Timed out. 2026-11-05 (168f55d): won with the dies-with tactic; Black Lady died in 1.2 minutes.

**Why it blocks:** The Oak never pulls itself (AggroLevel 0) and stands inside its tree; the raid fought Black Lady away from her spawn so she leashed and healed.

**Proposed tactic:** Skip the Oak: it dies when Black Lady dies. Fight Black Lady where she stands (don't drag her), one party on Ogresses. PR #77 already fights the Oak from a walkable approach and sets it aside after three empty pulls.

Rules of Ancient Black Oak (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/MarfachCaverns/AncientBlackOak.cs`):

- **immune_while** (verified): Takes no damage while it is drawn past its tether. `AncientBlackOak.cs:54`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Fight inside the tether: stop hitting it once it is drawn out and lead it home (AutonomousTetherReset already does this).
- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health `AncientBlackOak.cs:161`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **linked_pack** (verified): When pulled it puts every living NPC with PackageID "AncientOakBaf" in the region on its aggro list. The release world database has no such NPC, so today it brings nobody. `AncientBlackOak.cs:176`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Nothing to do today; if the world gets AncientOakBaf mobs, clear or pull them first.

Rules of Black Lady (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/MarfachCaverns/BlackLady.cs`):

- **immune_while** (verified): Takes no damage while it is drawn past its tether. `BlackLady.cs:24`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Fight inside the tether: stop hitting it once it is drawn out and lead it home (AutonomousTetherReset already does this).

### 3. Nosdoden (Trollheim): nine waves of 12-18 adds

**Run evidence:** overnight-2026-11-04 checks 5 and day run: Defeated twice in a row at Nosdoden (support 80, then 104; and 132 at 135% size), Special Forces joined each time.

**Why it blocks:** Each 10% step spawns 12-18 level 60 adds at his feet; a big raid's damage pushes several steps at once and the waves stack.

**Proposed tactic:** Pace the damage one 10% step at a time; at each threshold stop on him and AoE the wave down; healers stay with the tank.

Rules of Nosdoden (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Trollheim/Nosdoden.cs`):

To play it with bots: a dead bot's ghost fights the raid; kill it, or res the bot (the ghost dies when its owner lives again).
- **adds** (verified): SpawnEssences: about 12-18 essence of fallen (NosdodenSummonedAdds, StandardMobBrain-based, aggro range 1,500) around him at each of nine thresholds (90, 80, ... 10%). `Nosdoden.cs:674`. *needs new raid AI*: Push him one 10% step at a time: stop damage on him at each threshold, AoE classes and two parties burn the wave down at his feet, healers keep the tank up, then the next step. Don't let three waves stack.

### 4. Xanxicar (Crystal Cave): stomps and champions

**Run evidence:** overnight notes, day run 2026-11-05: Defeated at Xanxicar with support 199 (required 150), Special Forces joined, one named kill.

**Why it blocks:** Four announced point-blank stomps on everyone around him plus an endless chain of level 65 epic champions from 50%; the throw and glare skip bots, so the danger is the stomp and the champions.

**Proposed tactic:** Casters and healers outside the stomp radius on each announcement; one party per champion, killed away from the tank; healers top up before 80/60/40/20%.

Rules of Xanxicar (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/AvalonCity/CrystalCave/Xanxicar.cs`):

- **adds** (verified): At 50%: one Xanxicarian Champion (GameEpicNPC, level 65); after it dies another follows every 15-25 s while he fights. `Xanxicar.cs:382`. *needs new raid AI*: One party holds and kills each Champion away from the main tank. The route treats an epic NPC as its own encounter, not his add.
- **adds** (verified): Spawns XanxicarianChampion: on a timer (Util.Random(15000, 25000) ms); count 1 spawn call in SpawnMoreAdds `Xanxicar.cs:396`. *needs new raid AI*: Its adds are epic NPCs: the route treats them as separate encounters, not adds. Assign parties to them as they spawn.
- **phase** (verified): At 80/60/40/20% health: announces and casts Xanxicar's Stomp, a point-blank AoE on everything around him (1 s after the announcement). `Xanxicar.cs:337`. *needs new raid AI*: On the announcement casters, healers and archers step out of the stomp radius; melee eat it with full health. Healers top up before each threshold.
- **phase** (verified): At 50% health (Think): starts timer SpawnMoreAdds; calls SpawnAdds `Xanxicar.cs:357`. *needs new raid AI*: Know the threshold: get ready for what it starts (adds, a big hit) before pushing it past.
- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health, adds cleared `Xanxicar.cs:295`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **teleport** (verified): ThrowPlayer: every 20-40 s a random real player within 2,500 (not his target) is moved to 32338,32387,16539 ('hurled into the air'). `Xanxicar.cs:224`. *off today*: Off for bots today: it picks only real players (GameBot is a GameNPC, not a GamePlayer). To play it: a thrown bot walks back and rejoins its party.

### 5. Galladoria's last room: the four primals before Olcasgean

### 7. Princes Ba'alorien and Asmoien (Darkness Falls, no raid AI)

**Run evidence:** overnight-2026-11-04 checks 2-3: Albion and Hibernia Darkness Falls raids each Defeated after two wipes at Prince Ba'alorien.

**Why it blocks:** They eat a pet every 5 s and heal by its health; raids bring many pet classes.

**Proposed tactic:** Pet classes park pets out of reach (more than 2,000) for the princes. Darkness Falls has no raid AI; this is a proposal only.

Rules of Prince Ba'alorien (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/DarknessFalls/PrinceBaalorien.cs`):

- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health (waits until 8 s out of combat: EpicEncounterMechanics.FightIsOver) `PrinceBaalorien.cs:98`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **pet_devour** (verified): Every 5 s (PetDevourIntervalMilliseconds) the nearest summoned pet within 2,000 (a player's or a bot's, never a bot: EpicEncounterMechanics.NearestDevourablePet) dies and he heals by its maximum health. `PrinceBaalorien.cs:109`. *needs new raid AI*: Pet classes leave or park their pets more than 2,000 away before the pull and fight without them.

Rules of Prince Asmoien (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/DarknessFalls/PrinceAsmoien.cs`):

- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health (waits until 8 s out of combat: EpicEncounterMechanics.FightIsOver) `PrinceAsmoien.cs:99`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **pet_devour** (verified): Every 5 s (PetDevourIntervalMilliseconds) the nearest summoned pet within 2,000 (a player's or a bot's, never a bot: EpicEncounterMechanics.NearestDevourablePet) dies and he heals by its maximum health. `PrinceAsmoien.cs:110`. *needs new raid AI*: Pet classes leave or park their pets more than 2,000 away before the pull.

### 8. Fornfrusenen and Icelord Agmundr (Tuscaran Glacier)

**Run evidence:** overnight-2026-11-04 checks 2-3: Midgard epic wiped at Fornfrusenen 22:57, then twice at Icelord Agmundr (00:24, 01:02) and was Defeated.

**Why it blocks:** Fornfrusenen takes no damage from anyone more than 200 from him (casters and archers do nothing) and his shards only from within 400 of their point; Agmundr brings his whole BAF pack with boosted stats and a Cold DD (650, radius 500).

**Proposed tactic:** Fornfrusenen: melee only, casters on the shards from close in. Agmundr: pull his pack to the raid and clear it first.

Rules of Fornfrusenen (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/TuscarianGlacier/Fornfrusonen.cs`):

- **immune_while** (verified): Takes no damage from attackers further than 200 from it (only close attackers hurt it). `Fornfrusonen.cs:104`. *needs new raid AI*: Fight it up close: casters and archers move in to the allowed distance or switch to the adds.
- **immune_while** (verified): Takes no damage from attackers further than 400 from point. `Fornfrusonen.cs:233`. *needs new raid AI*: Attack only from where the script allows (see the condition).

- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health, adds cleared (waits until 8 s out of combat: EpicEncounterMechanics.FightIsOver) `Fornfrusonen.cs:161`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **reset** (verified): Resets while drawn past its tether: aggro cleared, back to full health `Fornfrusonen.cs:184`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **reset** (verified): Resets after 30 s out of combat: aggro cleared, full health `Fornfrusonen.cs:189`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).

### 9. Aidon the Archwizard (Hall of the Corrupt)

**Run evidence:** overnight-2026-11-04 check 5: Albion Hall of the Corrupt wiped at Aidon 03:25 (living 13 of 56) and 03:58, Defeated. Again 2026-11-05 (168f55d): 22:38 switch to Aidon without a muster, 31 engaged / 31 fallen at 99%, counted as a wipe with 56 living; the next muster on the ramp gathered 0-2 of 75. Later the same night (server.log 22:50-23:16) the regroup filled (FINAL_ENGAGE 22:58:13 75/75, then 23:00 and 23:02 106/53) but all three pulls walked to the front left from the 22:38 switch (31686,35683,14888) while he stood at home (31353,37634,14873); the closest raider ended 1,979 away and REALM_EXPEDITION_PULL_UNREACHED set him aside at 23:04. The front is now resolved again from his home (REALM_EXPEDITION_FRONT_REFRESHED).

**Why it blocks:** A permanent damage shield, a 500 Heat AoE every 10-15 s, and four illusions (5,000 health, 300 AoE each) that come back every 30-45 s once all are dead; tether 1,500.

**Proposed tactic:** Built in step 3: the early pull is answered by the whole raid (pull back to the muster, he leashes and resets), the muster is measured from his home, and the raid kills three illusions and leaves the last alive, then fights him at home.

Rules of Aidon the Archwizard (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/HallOfTheCorrupt/AidonTheArchwizard.cs`):

- **immune_while** (verified): Takes no damage while it is drawn past its tether. `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:41`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Fight inside the tether: stop hitting it once it is drawn out and lead it home (AutonomousTetherReset already does this).
- **reflect** (verified): Has a damage shield / reflect spell: Aidon's Damage Shield (DamageShield, damage 100, target SELF) `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:398`. *needs new raid AI*: Check who it punishes; switch damage type or attacker if it reflects.
- **adds** (verified): Spawns 4 kinds of add (Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyAir), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyFire), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyEarth), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyIce)): on a timer (Util.Random(30000, 45000) ms) `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:279`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Kill the adds as they spawn (the route already puts script-spawned adds near the front first).

- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health, adds cleared `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:205`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).

### 10. Conservator and the Organic-Energy Mechanism (Galladoria)

**Run evidence:** Code history (RealmRaidDungeonRoute): run 25 Conservator wiped the raid twice (45%, 42%); 2026-10-20 the Organic-Energy Mechanism wiped 152 of 156 and the regroup did worse.

**Why it blocks:** Raw damage plus adds every 10 s (3-5 bottom feeders) and a damage shield on the Mechanism; Conservator's stand-near-the-spawn rule is off today.

**Proposed tactic:** Add party on the feeders; melee respect the Mechanism's shield; fight Conservator at his spawn.

Level 87, 250,000 health, the final boss. Asleep until all four primals are dead. Spawns a copy of himself (Olcasgean2, a second 250,000-health body) when he wakes. The split-position puzzle (raiders must stand at two points or he spams his DD) and his throws are switched off for bots.

### Hall of the Corrupt (region 277)

#### Aidon the Archwizard (`AidonTheArchwizard`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/HallOfTheCorrupt/AidonTheArchwizard.cs` lines 10-143. level 75; 40,000 health; npc template 7721; raids: dungeon-albion-corrupt (final); raid AI: generic dungeon route + tactics (EpicEncounterTactics).

Level 75, 40,000 health, 30/40% resists, tether 1,500 (immune past it). What stops damage: only the tether; his permanent Damage Shield (Heat, 100 per hit back on melee attackers, recast whenever it is not on him) does not reduce damage taken, so he is killable by design; no damage type bypasses anything and no dispel is needed (he recasts it at once). What kills raids: his four illusions (5,000 health each, spawned at fixed spots the first time he is attacked) each cast a 300 area hit in 350 around their target every brain turn, and he casts Aidon's Fire (500 Heat, radius 350) every 10-15 s. A new set of illusions comes 30-45 s after all four are dead, never while one lives. No player-only mechanic is needed to beat him, so no switch has to flip.

| Kind | Rule | Where | Confidence | Switch | Difficulty | Proposed tactic |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| immune_while / out_of_tether | Takes no damage while it is drawn past its tether. | `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:41` | read, verified |  | easy for the current raid AI | Fight inside the tether: stop hitting it once it is drawn out and lead it home (AutonomousTetherReset already does this). |
| reflect | Has a damage shield / reflect spell: Aidon's Damage Shield (DamageShield, damage 100, target SELF) | `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:398` | read, verified |  | needs new raid AI | Check who it punishes; switch damage type or attacker if it reflects. |
| adds | Spawns 4 kinds of add (Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyAir), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyFire), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyEarth), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyIce)): on a timer (Util.Random(30000, 45000) ms) | `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:279` | inferred, verified |  | easy for the current raid AI | Kill the adds as they spawn (the route already puts script-spawned adds near the front first). |
| adds | Spawns 4 kinds of add (Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyFire), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyIce), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyAir), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyEarth)): from SpawnCopies | `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:323` | inferred, verified |  | easy for the current raid AI | Kill the adds as they spawn (the route already puts script-spawned adds near the front first). |
| reset | Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health, adds cleared | `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:205` | read, verified |  | easy for the current raid AI | Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it). |

#### Scurceol Hyrde (`ScurceolHyrde`)

Immune while any of his four Lyft Miht orbs lives. The orbs (level 90, 15,000 health, 40% melee and 50% magic resists, AF 250) float 450-790 above the room floor at its four corners (51236,20849 / 51316,19228 / 52702,19214 / 52713,20841; floor about 16964), never attack (StartAttack is empty), have a 250 aggro range and RespawnInterval -1. Once dead they stay dead: his reset does not bring them back, only his own respawn (SpawnOrbs runs in AddToWorld). He gains 60 Strength while any orb lives.

#### Nosdoden (`Nosdoden`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Trollheim/Nosdoden.cs` lines 14-356. level 80; 100,000 health; npc template 60164545; raids: dungeon-midgard-trollheim (final); raid AI: generic dungeon route + tactics (EpicEncounterTactics).

Level 80, 100,000 health. Every 10% from 90% down to 10% he spawns a wave of about 12-18 'essence of fallen' (level 60-62, 2,500 health, aggro 1,500) at his feet: nine waves, roughly 110-160 adds in one fight. His Acidic cloud (Body DoT 150 for 60 s, radius 500) and Call of Void (Cold 1,100, radius 550) go on random real players within 2,000 only (GetPlayersInRadius), so bots never draw them. Spirits of the fallen are made only from dead real players.
